===== FILE: ./src/commands/anime/publisher.ts =====
import type { Env } from '../../types/env';
import type { AniListMedia } from '../../types/anime';
import type { SessionRow } from '../../lib/dba-session';
import type { FileToCommit } from '../../lib/github';
import { githubCommitMultipleFiles } from '../../lib/github';
import { getCharCache } from '../../lib/dba-characters';
import { getEpCache } from '../../lib/dba-episodes';
import { getAllVoiceActors } from '../../lib/dba-voice-actors';
import {
  chainRelations,
  type UnifiedCharacter,
  type UnifiedVoiceActor,
  type UnifiedRelation,
} from '../../services/qimochi-chain-extras';
import { buildMetadataYaml } from '../../services/qimochi-yaml';
import { filterFranchises } from '../../lib/franchises';
import { chunkArray, slugify, stripHtml, looksIndonesian } from '../../lib/utils';
import { askAI } from '../../services/ai';
import { safeFetch } from '../../lib/dba-common';

const CHAR_PART_SIZE = 50;
const EP_PART_SIZE = 12;
const MAX_ACTORS_PER_FILE = 500;

interface BuildFilesResult {
  files: FileToCommit[];
  slug: string;
  body: string;
  counts: {
    metadata: boolean;
    characters: number;
    episodes: number;
    franchises: number;
    actors: number;
  };
}

function resolveSlug(session: SessionRow): string {
  return slugify(session.title);
}

function sanitizeCharacter(c: UnifiedCharacter): {
  name: string;
  role: string;
  voiceActors: string[];
  nameNative?: string;
  image?: string;
} {
  const out: {
    name: string;
    role: string;
    voiceActors: string[];
    nameNative?: string;
    image?: string;
  } = {
    name: c.name?.trim() || 'Unknown',
    role:
      c.role === 'main' || c.role === 'supporting' || c.role === 'background'
        ? c.role
        : 'background',
    voiceActors: Array.isArray(c.voiceActors) ? c.voiceActors.filter(Boolean) : [],
  };
  if (c.nameNative) out.nameNative = c.nameNative.trim();
  if (c.image && /^https?:\/\//.test(c.image)) out.image = c.image.trim();
  return out;
}

function sanitizeActor(va: {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  nameNative: string | null;
  image: string | null;
  defaultLanguage: string | null;
}): {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  nameNative?: string;
  image?: string;
  defaultLanguage?: string;
} {
  const out: {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    nameNative?: string;
    image?: string;
    defaultLanguage?: string;
  } = {
    id: va.id.trim().toLowerCase() || 'unknown',
    name: va.name.trim() || 'Unknown',
  };
  if (va.nameNative) out.nameNative = va.nameNative.trim();
  if (va.image && /^https?:\/\//.test(va.image)) out.image = va.image.trim();
  if (va.defaultLanguage) out.defaultLanguage = va.defaultLanguage.trim();
  return out;
}

async function rewriteSynopsis(
  env: Env,
  title: string,
  originalSynopsis: string
): Promise<string | null> {
  if (!originalSynopsis || originalSynopsis.length < 30) return null;

  const prompt =
    `Tulis ulang sinopsis anime berikut menjadi bahasa Indonesia yang natural.\n\n` +
    `Judul: ${title}\n\n` +
    `Sinopsis referensi:\n${originalSynopsis.slice(0, 2000)}\n\n` +
    `ATURAN:\n` +
    `- Tulis sebagai sinopsis baru, BUKAN terjemahan literal\n` +
    `- Bahasa Indonesia natural dan mengalir\n` +
    `- 2-3 paragraf pendek\n` +
    `- Jangan spoiler\n` +
    `- Jangan tambahkan info yang tidak ada di referensi\n` +
    `- Langsung mulai dari tokoh utama atau setting\n\n` +
    `Output hanya sinopsis, tanpa penjelasan tambahan.`;

  const { data } = await safeFetch(
    () => askAI(env, prompt, { maxTokens: 700, temperature: 0.6, smart: true }),
    12000
  );

  if (data && data.length > 50) return data.trim();
  return null;
}

async function resolveBody(
  env: Env,
  session: SessionRow,
  media: AniListMedia
): Promise<string> {
  if (session.summary && session.summary.trim().length > 50) {
    return session.summary.trim();
  }

  const raw = media.description ?? '';
  if (raw.length < 30) {
    return '> ⚠️ Sinopsis belum tersedia. Silakan isi manual.';
  }

  const clean = stripHtml(raw);
  if (looksIndonesian(clean)) return clean;

  const aiBody = await rewriteSynopsis(env, session.title, clean);
  return aiBody ?? clean;
}

export async function buildFiles(
  env: Env,
  session: SessionRow
): Promise<BuildFilesResult> {
  const slug = resolveSlug(session);
  const files: FileToCommit[] = [];

  let media: AniListMedia | null = null;
  if (session.metadata) {
    try {
      media = JSON.parse(session.metadata) as AniListMedia;
    } catch {}
  }
  if (!media) throw new Error('Session tidak punya metadata');

  const body = await resolveBody(env, session, media);
  const yaml = buildMetadataYaml({
    media,
    malId: session.mal_id ?? null,
    kitsuId: session.kitsu_id ?? null,
  });

  files.push({
    path: `src/content/anime/${slug}.md`,
    content: `${yaml}\n\n${body}\n`,
    itemCount: 1,
  });

  const counts = {
    metadata: true,
    characters: 0,
    episodes: 0,
    franchises: 0,
    actors: 0,
  };

  /* Characters */
  const charCache = await getCharCache(env.DB, session.session_id);
  if (charCache && charCache.chars.length > 0) {
    const sanitized = charCache.chars.map(sanitizeCharacter);
    const chunks = chunkArray(sanitized, CHAR_PART_SIZE);
    let cursor = 1;
    for (const chunk of chunks) {
      const start = cursor;
      const end = cursor + chunk.length - 1;
      files.push({
        path: `data/anime/${slug}/characters/${start}-${end}.json`,
        content: JSON.stringify(chunk, null, 2) + '\n',
        itemCount: chunk.length,
      });
      cursor = end + 1;
      counts.characters += chunk.length;
    }
  }

  /* Episodes */
  const epCache = await getEpCache(env.DB, session.session_id);
  if (epCache && epCache.episodes.length > 0) {
    const sorted = [...epCache.episodes].sort((a, b) => a.number - b.number);
    const chunks = chunkArray(sorted, EP_PART_SIZE);
    for (const chunk of chunks) {
      const first = chunk[0];
      const last = chunk[chunk.length - 1];
      if (!first || !last) continue;
      files.push({
        path: `data/anime/${slug}/episodes/${first.number}-${last.number}.json`,
        content: JSON.stringify(chunk, null, 2) + '\n',
        itemCount: chunk.length,
      });
      counts.episodes += chunk.length;
    }
  }

  /* Franchises */
  if (session.mal_id) {
    const { data: result } = await safeFetch(
      () =>
        chainRelations({
          malId: session.mal_id!,
          kitsuId: session.kitsu_id,
          title: session.title,
        }),
      8000
    );
    if (result && result.data && result.data.length > 0) {
      const filtered = filterFranchises(result.data);
      if (filtered.length > 0) {
        files.push({
          path: `data/anime/${slug}/franchises.json`,
          content: JSON.stringify(filtered, null, 2) + '\n',
          itemCount: filtered.length,
        });
        counts.franchises = filtered.length;
      }
    }
  }

  /* Actors */
  const vas = await getAllVoiceActors(env.DB);
  if (vas.length > 0) {
    const sanitized = vas.map(sanitizeActor);
    const groups = new Map<string, typeof sanitized>();
    for (const va of sanitized) {
      const first = (va.id.charAt(0) || '').toLowerCase();
      const letter = /^[a-z]$/.test(first) ? first : '_';
      if (!groups.has(letter)) groups.set(letter, []);
      groups.get(letter)!.push(va);
    }
    for (const [letter, items] of groups) {
      items.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
      const chunks = chunkArray(items, MAX_ACTORS_PER_FILE);
      for (let i = 0; i < chunks.length; i++) {
        const chunk = chunks[i]!;
        const path =
          chunks.length === 1
            ? `data/actors/${letter}.json`
            : `data/actors/${letter}-${i + 1}.json`;
        files.push({
          path,
          content: JSON.stringify(chunk, null, 2) + '\n',
          itemCount: chunk.length,
        });
        counts.actors += chunk.length;
      }
    }
  }

  return { files, slug, body, counts };
}

export interface PublishResult {
  ok: boolean;
  sha?: string;
  commitUrl?: string;
  error?: string;
  slug: string;
  filesCount: number;
  counts: BuildFilesResult['counts'];
}

export async function publishAnime(
  env: Env,
  session: SessionRow
): Promise<PublishResult> {
  let built: BuildFilesResult;
  try {
    built = await buildFiles(env, session);
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: (err as Error).message,
      slug: '',
      filesCount: 0,
      counts: {
        metadata: false,
        characters: 0,
        episodes: 0,
        franchises: 0,
        actors: 0,
      },
    };
  }

  const result = await githubCommitMultipleFiles(
    env,
    built.files,
    `feat: publish ${built.slug}`
  );

  return {
    ok: result.ok,
    sha: result.sha,
    commitUrl: result.commitUrl,
    error: result.error,
    slug: built.slug,
    filesCount: built.files.length,
    counts: built.counts,
  };
}

export type { UnifiedRelation, UnifiedVoiceActor };
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: ./src/commands/anime/ui.ts =====
import { InlineKeyboard } from 'grammy';
import { escapeHtml } from '../../lib/utils';
import type { SessionRow } from '../../lib/dba-session';

export function buildKeyboard(sessionId: string): InlineKeyboard {
  return new InlineKeyboard()
    .text('📋 Metadata', `an:m:${sessionId}`)
    .text('👥 Characters', `an:c:${sessionId}`)
    .row()
    .text('🎬 Episodes', `an:e:${sessionId}`)
    .text('🔗 Franchises', `an:f:${sessionId}`)
    .row()
    .text('🎤 Voice Actors', `an:v:${sessionId}`)
    .text('📝 Summary', `an:s:${sessionId}`)
    .row()
    .text('📤 Publish ke yukio-data', `an:pub:${sessionId}`)
    .row()
    .text('❌ Batal', `an:x:${sessionId}`);
}

export function buildPreviewText(session: SessionRow): string {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  lines.push(`<b>${escapeHtml(session.title)}</b>`);
  lines.push('');
  if (session.year) lines.push(`📅 ${escapeHtml(session.year)}`);
  if (session.type) lines.push(`🎬 ${escapeHtml(session.type)}`);
  if (session.studio) lines.push(`🏢 ${escapeHtml(session.studio)}`);
  if (session.source) lines.push(`📡 ${escapeHtml(session.source)}`);
  lines.push('');
  lines.push('<i>Klik section untuk fetch data, lalu 📤 Publish.</i>');
  return lines.join('\n');
}

export function buildPublishKeyboard(sessionId: string): InlineKeyboard {
  return new InlineKeyboard()
    .text('✅ Push ke yukio-data', `an:push:${sessionId}`)
    .text('❌ Batal', `an:x:${sessionId}`);
}
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: ./src/commands/batch.ts =====

// src/commands/batch.ts
import type { CommandDefinition } from './registry';
import type { Context } from 'grammy';
import { InlineKeyboard, type Bot } from 'grammy';
import type { Env } from '../types/env';
import type { EpisodeObject } from '../types/anime';
import { getCache, setCache, deleteCache } from '../lib/cache';
import { githubGetFile, githubListDir, githubCommitFile } from '../lib/github';
import {
  BATCH_MAX,
  looksLikeHtml,
  decodeInput,
  buildEpisodeObject,
  fetchUrlViaProxy,
} from '../services/decode-core';

const WIZARD_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;

type SiteKey = 'lexanime' | 'animesub' | 'samehadaku';
type WizardSite = SiteKey | 'custom';

interface WizardState {
  step: 'awaiting_slug' | 'awaiting_source' | 'awaiting_range' | 'ready';
  slug: string | null;
  sourceUrl: string | null;
  sourceSite: WizardSite | null;
  /** Untuk site known: slug di situs sumber. Untuk custom: hostname saja. */
  sourceSlug: string | null;
  /** Khusus custom: URL template dengan placeholder `{n}`. */
  customPattern: string | null;
  rangeStart: number | null;
  rangeEnd: number | null;
}

interface SiteConfig {
  host: string;
  episodePath: (slug: string, n: number) => string;
}

const WIZARD_SITES: Record<SiteKey, SiteConfig> = {
  lexanime: {
    host: 'https://lexanime.web.id',
    episodePath: (slug, n) => `/tonton/${slug}/episode-${n}-sub-indo`,
  },
  animesub: {
    host: 'https://animesub.web.id',
    episodePath: (slug, n) => `/tonton/${slug}/episode-${n}-sub-indo`,
  },
  samehadaku: {
    host: 'https://samehadaku.li',
    episodePath: (slug, n) => `/${slug}-episode-${n}-subtitle-indonesia/`,
  },
};

type ParsedSource =
  | { kind: 'known'; site: SiteKey; slug: string }
  | { kind: 'custom'; pattern: string; host: string };

/**
 * Auto-detect pola episode di URL → template dengan `{n}`.
 * Return null kalau tidak ada pola yang bisa dipakai.
 */
function resolveCustomPattern(
  input: string
): { pattern: string; host: string } | null {
  const trimmed = input.trim();
  if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(trimmed)) return null;

  // Hostname — substitute {n} biar URL valid untuk parsing.
  let host: string;
  try {
    host = new URL(trimmed.replace(/\{n\}/g, '1')).hostname.toLowerCase();
  } catch {
    return null;
  }

  // 1) User sudah kasih {n} eksplisit
  if (/\{n\}/.test(trimmed)) {
    return { pattern: trimmed, host };
  }

  // 2) "episode-5", "ep_12", "EP.3", dst.
  const m1 = trimmed.match(/^(.*?)(episode|ep|eps)([-_.]?)(\d+)(.*)$/i);
  if (m1) {
    const prefix = m1[1] ?? '';
    const keyword = m1[2] ?? 'episode';
    const sep = m1[3] ?? '';
    const suffix = m1[5] ?? '';
    return { pattern: `${prefix}${keyword}${sep}{n}${suffix}`, host };
  }

  // 3) URL diakhiri segmen angka: /watch/foo/12, /watch/foo/12/, /watch/foo/12?x=1
  const m2 = trimmed.match(/^(.*\/)(\d+)(\D.*)?$/);
  if (m2) {
    const prefix = m2[1] ?? '';
    const suffix = m2[3] ?? '';
    return { pattern: `${prefix}{n}${suffix}`, host };
  }

  return null;
}

function parseSourceUrl(url: string): ParsedSource | null {
  const trimmed = url.trim();
  if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(trimmed)) return null;

  try {
    const u = new URL(trimmed.replace(/\{n\}/g, '1'));
    const host = u.hostname.toLowerCase();

    if (host.includes('lexanime')) {
      const slug = u.pathname.match(/\/tonton\/([^/]+)/)?.[1];
      if (slug) return { kind: 'known', site: 'lexanime', slug };
    }
    if (host.includes('animesub')) {
      const slug = u.pathname.match(/\/tonton\/([^/]+)/)?.[1];
      if (slug) return { kind: 'known', site: 'animesub', slug };
    }
    if (host.includes('samehadaku')) {
      const s1 = u.pathname.match(/\/anime\/([^/]+)/)?.[1];
      if (s1) return { kind: 'known', site: 'samehadaku', slug: s1 };
      const s2 = u.pathname.match(
        /\/([^/]+?)-episode-\d+-subtitle-indonesia/
      )?.[1];
      if (s2) return { kind: 'known', site: 'samehadaku', slug: s2 };
    }
  } catch {
    return null;
  }

  const custom = resolveCustomPattern(trimmed);
  if (custom) {
    return { kind: 'custom', pattern: custom.pattern, host: custom.host };
  }
  return null;
}

function escapeHtml(s: string): string {
  return s
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
}

async function startBatchWizard(ctx: Context, env: Env): Promise<void> {
  const userId = ctx.from?.id;
  if (!userId) return;

  const state: WizardState = {
    step: 'awaiting_slug',
    slug: null,
    sourceUrl: null,
    sourceSite: null,
    sourceSlug: null,
    customPattern: null,
    rangeStart: null,
    rangeEnd: null,
  };
  await setCache(env.DB, `bw:${userId}`, state, WIZARD_TTL_MS);

  await ctx.reply(
    '📦 <b>Batch Wizard</b>\n\n' +
      '<b>Step 1/3</b> · Kirim <b>slug anime</b>.\n\n' +
      '<i>Contoh: <code>jujutsu-kaisen</code></i>\n\n' +
      'Bot akan cek dulu ke repo yukio-data.',
    {
      parse_mode: 'HTML',
      link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
      reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard().text('❌ Batal', 'bw:cancel'),
    }
  );
}

export async function handleBatchWizardText(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env
): Promise<boolean> {
  const userId = ctx.from?.id;
  if (!userId) return false;

  const text = ctx.message?.text?.trim() ?? '';
  if (!text || text.startsWith('/')) return false;

  const state = await getCache<WizardState>(env.DB, `bw:${userId}`);
  if (!state) return false;

  if (state.step === 'awaiting_slug') {
    return handleWizardSlug(ctx, env, userId, state, text);
  }
  if (state.step === 'awaiting_source') {
    return handleWizardSource(ctx, env, userId, state, text);
  }
  if (state.step === 'awaiting_range') {
    return handleWizardRange(ctx, env, userId, state, text);
  }
  return false;
}

async function handleWizardSlug(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  userId: number,
  state: WizardState,
  text: string
): Promise<boolean> {
  const slug = text.toLowerCase().trim();

  if (!/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*[a-z0-9]$/.test(slug)) {
    await ctx.reply(
      '❌ Slug invalid. Format: <code>lowercase-with-dash</code>\n\nCoba lagi:',
      { parse_mode: 'HTML' }
    );
    return true;
  }

  const loading = await ctx.reply(
    `🔍 Cek <code>${escapeHtml(slug)}</code> ke yukio-data...`,
    { parse_mode: 'HTML' }
  );

  let file: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof githubGetFile>> = null;
  try {
    file = await githubGetFile(env, `src/content/anime/${slug}.md`);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[BatchWizard] check failed:', err);
  }

  await ctx.api.deleteMessage(ctx.chat!.id, loading.message_id).catch(() => {});

  if (!file) {
    await ctx.reply(
      `❌ <b>Slug tidak ditemukan di yukio-data</b>\n\n` +
        `Path: <code>src/content/anime/${escapeHtml(slug)}.md</code>\n\n` +
        `Coba slug lain:`,
      {
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
        reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard().text('❌ Batal', 'bw:cancel'),
      }
    );
    return true;
  }

  state.step = 'awaiting_source';
  state.slug = slug;
  await setCache(env.DB, `bw:${userId}`, state, WIZARD_TTL_MS);

  await ctx.reply(
    `✅ <b>Slug ditemukan!</b>\n\n` +
      `<b>Step 2/3</b> · Kirim <b>URL source</b>.\n\n` +
      `🎬 Repo: <code>${escapeHtml(slug)}</code>\n\n` +
      `<b>A. Situs dikenal (slug otomatis):</b>\n` +
      `• <code>https://lexanime.web.id/tonton/{slug}/</code>\n` +
      `• <code>https://animesub.web.id/tonton/{slug}/</code>\n` +
      `• <code>https://samehadaku.li/anime/{slug}/</code>\n\n` +
      `<b>B. Situs lain (custom):</b>\n` +
      `• Kirim URL episode dengan placeholder <code>{n}</code>:\n` +
      `  <code>https://situs.com/foo/episode-{n}</code>\n` +
      `• Atau kirim contoh URL episode (bot auto-detect):\n` +
      `  <code>https://situs.com/foo/episode-1</code>\n` +
      `  <code>https://situs.com/watch/foo/1</code>`,
    {
      parse_mode: 'HTML',
      link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
      reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard().text('❌ Batal', 'bw:cancel'),
    }
  );
  return true;
}

async function handleWizardSource(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  userId: number,
  state: WizardState,
  text: string
): Promise<boolean> {
  const parsed = parseSourceUrl(text);

  if (!parsed) {
    await ctx.reply(
      '❌ URL tidak dikenali.\n\n' +
        '<b>Situs dikenal:</b>\n' +
        '• <code>https://lexanime.web.id/tonton/{slug}/</code>\n' +
        '• <code>https://animesub.web.id/tonton/{slug}/</code>\n' +
        '• <code>https://samehadaku.li/anime/{slug}/</code>\n\n' +
        '<b>Custom URL:</b> pastikan ada nomor episode, atau pakai placeholder:\n' +
        '• <code>https://situs.com/foo/episode-{n}</code>\n' +
        '• <code>https://situs.com/foo/episode-1</code>\n' +
        '• <code>https://situs.com/watch/foo/1</code>\n\n' +
        'Coba lagi:',
      {
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
        reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard().text('❌ Batal', 'bw:cancel'),
      }
    );
    return true;
  }

  state.step = 'awaiting_range';
  state.sourceUrl = text.trim();

  if (parsed.kind === 'known') {
    state.sourceSite = parsed.site;
    state.sourceSlug = parsed.slug;
    state.customPattern = null;
  } else {
    state.sourceSite = 'custom';
    state.sourceSlug = parsed.host;
    state.customPattern = parsed.pattern;
  }

  await setCache(env.DB, `bw:${userId}`, state, WIZARD_TTL_MS);

  const siteLabel =
    parsed.kind === 'known' ? parsed.site : `custom (${parsed.host})`;

  let patternLabel: string;
  if (parsed.kind === 'known') {
    const cfg = WIZARD_SITES[parsed.site];
    patternLabel = `${cfg.host}${cfg.episodePath(parsed.slug, 1)}`;
  } else {
    patternLabel = parsed.pattern;
  }

  await ctx.reply(
    `✅ <b>Source OK!</b>\n\n` +
      `<b>Step 3/3</b> · Kirim <b>range episode</b>.\n\n` +
      `🎬 Repo: <code>${escapeHtml(state.slug ?? '?')}</code>\n` +
      `🔗 Site: <b>${escapeHtml(siteLabel)}</b>\n` +
      `📼 URL sample ep 1: <code>${escapeHtml(patternLabel)}</code>\n\n` +
      `<b>Contoh range:</b>\n` +
      `• <code>1-6</code> (ep 1 sampai 6)\n` +
      `• <code>4</code> (hanya ep 4)\n\n` +
      `<i>Kalau file existing sudah ada (misal 1-3), bot akan skip.</i>`,
    {
      parse_mode: 'HTML',
      link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
      reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard().text('❌ Batal', 'bw:cancel'),
    }
  );
  return true;
}

async function handleWizardRange(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  userId: number,
  state: WizardState,
  text: string
): Promise<boolean> {
  const m = text.trim().match(/^(\d+)\s*(?:-\s*(\d+))?$/);
  if (!m) {
    await ctx.reply(
      '❌ Format range salah. Contoh: <code>1-6</code> atau <code>4</code>\n\nCoba lagi:',
      { parse_mode: 'HTML' }
    );
    return true;
  }

  const start = parseInt(m[1] ?? '0', 10);
  const end = m[2] ? parseInt(m[2], 10) : start;

  if (start < 1 || end < start || end - start + 1 > BATCH_MAX) {
    await ctx.reply(
      `❌ Range tidak valid (max ${BATCH_MAX} ep). Contoh: <code>1-6</code>`,
      { parse_mode: 'HTML' }
    );
    return true;
  }

  state.step = 'ready';
  state.rangeStart = start;
  state.rangeEnd = end;
  await setCache(env.DB, `bw:${userId}`, state, WIZARD_TTL_MS);

  const siteLabel = state.sourceSite ?? '-';
  const sourceLabel =
    state.sourceSite === 'custom'
      ? (state.customPattern ?? '-')
      : (state.sourceSlug ?? '-');

  await ctx.reply(
    `📋 <b>Konfirmasi Batch</b>\n\n` +
      `🎬 Repo: <code>${escapeHtml(state.slug ?? '?')}</code>\n` +
      `🔗 Site: <b>${escapeHtml(siteLabel)}</b>\n` +
      `📼 Source: <code>${escapeHtml(sourceLabel)}</code>\n` +
      `📊 Range: <b>${start}-${end}</b> (${end - start + 1} ep)\n\n` +
      `<i>Bot akan cek file existing di <code>episodes/streams/</code>, fetch yang belum ada, lalu merge.</i>`,
    {
      parse_mode: 'HTML',
      link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
      reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard()
        .text('🔄 Proses', 'bw:go')
        .text('❌ Batal', 'bw:cancel'),
    }
  );
  return true;
}

async function execBatchWizard(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  userId: number
): Promise<void> {
  const state = await getCache<WizardState>(env.DB, `bw:${userId}`);
  if (!state || state.step !== 'ready') {
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({
      text: '⏱️ Session kadaluarsa',
      show_alert: true,
    });
    return;
  }

  const slug = state.slug;
  const sourceSite = state.sourceSite;
  const rangeStart = state.rangeStart;
  const rangeEnd = state.rangeEnd;

  if (
    !slug ||
    !sourceSite ||
    rangeStart === null ||
    rangeEnd === null
  ) {
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({
      text: '⏱️ Session kadaluarsa',
      show_alert: true,
    });
    return;
  }

  await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '🔄 Memproses...' });

  const loadingMsgId = ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!;
  const chatId = ctx.chat!.id;
  const total = rangeEnd - rangeStart + 1;

  /* ── Build URL builder ───────────────────────────────── */
  let epUrlBuilder: (n: number) => string;

  if (sourceSite === 'custom') {
    const pattern = state.customPattern;
    if (!pattern) {
      await ctx.api
        .editMessageText(
          chatId,
          loadingMsgId,
          '❌ Pattern custom hilang. Ulangi /batch.',
          { parse_mode: 'HTML' }
        )
        .catch(() => {});
      return;
    }
    epUrlBuilder = (n) => pattern.replace(/\{n\}/g, String(n));
  } else {
    // TS narrow sourceSite → SiteKey
    const siteConfig = WIZARD_SITES[sourceSite];
    const sourceSlug = state.sourceSlug;
    if (!sourceSlug) {
      await ctx.api
        .editMessageText(
          chatId,
          loadingMsgId,
          '❌ Source slug hilang. Ulangi /batch.',
          { parse_mode: 'HTML' }
        )
        .catch(() => {});
      return;
    }
    epUrlBuilder = (n) =>
      `${siteConfig.host}${siteConfig.episodePath(sourceSlug, n)}`;
  }

  /* ── Read existing chunks ────────────────────────────── */
  const existing = new Map<number, EpisodeObject>();
  try {
    const dir = await githubListDir(env, `data/anime/${slug}/episodes/streams`);
    for (const item of dir) {
      if (item.type !== 'file' || !item.name.endsWith('.json')) continue;
      const file = await githubGetFile(
        env,
        `data/anime/${slug}/episodes/streams/${item.name}`
      );
      if (!file) continue;
      try {
        const eps = JSON.parse(file.content) as unknown;
        if (Array.isArray(eps)) {
          for (const ep of eps as EpisodeObject[]) {
            if (typeof ep.number === 'number') existing.set(ep.number, ep);
          }
        }
      } catch {
        /* ignore malformed */
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[BatchWizard] read existing failed:', err);
  }

  /* ── Fetch loop ──────────────────────────────────────── */
  const merged: EpisodeObject[] = [];
  const errors: { number: number; error: string }[] = [];
  const added: number[] = [];
  const skipped: number[] = [];

  for (let n = rangeStart; n <= rangeEnd; n++) {
    const cached = existing.get(n);
    if (cached) {
      merged.push(cached);
      skipped.push(n);
      continue;
    }

    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(
        chatId,
        loadingMsgId,
        `📦 <b>Batch ${rangeStart}-${rangeEnd}</b>\n\n` +
          `⏳ [${n - rangeStart + 1}/${total}] Episode ${n}...`,
        { parse_mode: 'HTML', link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true } }
      )
      .catch(() => {});

    const epUrl = epUrlBuilder(n);
    const { body, debug } = await fetchUrlViaProxy(env, epUrl);

    if (!body) {
      errors.push({ number: n, error: debug.error ?? 'fetch failed' });
      continue;
    }

    const sourceType: 'base64' | 'html' = looksLikeHtml(body) ? 'html' : 'base64';
    const processed = decodeInput(body, sourceType);
    if (!processed) {
      errors.push({ number: n, error: `no video URLs (${body.length} char)` });
      continue;
    }

    merged.push(buildEpisodeObject(processed.videos, n));
    added.push(n);
  }

  merged.sort((a, b) => a.number - b.number);

  const first = merged[0];
  const last = merged[merged.length - 1];
  if (!first || !last) {
    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(
        chatId,
        loadingMsgId,
        '❌ Tidak ada episode yang berhasil di-fetch.',
        { parse_mode: 'HTML' }
      )
      .catch(() => {});
    return;
  }

  const minEp = first.number;
  const maxEp = last.number;
  const combinedJson = JSON.stringify(merged, null, 2) + '\n';
  const totalUrls = merged.reduce(
    (sum, r) => sum + r.streams.reduce((s, q) => s + q.servers.length, 0),
    0
  );

  const targetPath = `data/anime/${slug}/episodes/streams/${minEp}-${maxEp}.json`;

  try {
    const result = await githubCommitFile(
      env,
      targetPath,
      combinedJson,
      `feat(streams): batch ${minEp}-${maxEp} for ${slug}`
    );

    if (!result.ok) {
      await ctx.api
        .editMessageText(
          chatId,
          loadingMsgId,
          `❌ <b>Gagal push</b>\n\n<code>${escapeHtml(result.error ?? 'unknown')}</code>`,
          { parse_mode: 'HTML' }
        )
        .catch(() => {});
      return;
    }

    const lines: string[] = [];
    lines.push(`✅ <b>Batch ${minEp}-${maxEp} selesai & pushed!</b>`);
    lines.push('');
    lines.push(`🎬 <code>${escapeHtml(slug)}</code>`);
    lines.push(`📁 <code>${escapeHtml(targetPath)}</code>`);
    lines.push('');
    lines.push(`📦 Total: <b>${merged.length}</b> ep (${minEp}-${maxEp})`);
    lines.push(`🎬 URL: <b>${totalUrls}</b>`);

    if (skipped.length > 0) {
      lines.push(`⏭️ Skip (existing): Ep ${skipped.join(', ')}`);
    }
    if (added.length > 0) {
      lines.push(`➕ Baru: Ep ${added.join(', ')}`);
    }

    if (errors.length > 0) {
      lines.push('');
      lines.push(`⚠️ Gagal: ${errors.length} ep`);
      for (const e of errors.slice(0, 3)) {
        lines.push(`   • Ep ${e.number}: ${escapeHtml(e.error.slice(0, 60))}`);
      }
    }

    lines.push('');
    lines.push(`🔗 Commit: <code>${result.sha?.slice(0, 7) ?? '?'}</code>`);
    lines.push('');
    lines.push('<i>Lanjut range berikutnya? Slug & source tetap.</i>');

    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(chatId, loadingMsgId, lines.join('\n'), {
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
        reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard()
          .text('🔄 Range Baru', 'bw:next')
          .text('➕ Anime Baru', 'bw:start')
          .row()
          .text('🏠 Close', 'bw:close'),
      })
      .catch(() => {});

    // Retain slug + source, reset range
    const nextState: WizardState = {
      step: 'awaiting_range',
      slug: state.slug,
      sourceUrl: state.sourceUrl,
      sourceSite: state.sourceSite,
      sourceSlug: state.sourceSlug,
      customPattern: state.customPattern,
      rangeStart: null,
      rangeEnd: null,
    };
    await setCache(env.DB, `bw:${userId}`, nextState, WIZARD_TTL_MS);
  } catch (err: unknown) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown';
    console.error('[BatchWizard] push failed:', err);
    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(
        chatId,
        loadingMsgId,
        `❌ <b>Error:</b> <code>${escapeHtml(msg.slice(0, 300))}</code>`,
        { parse_mode: 'HTML' }
      )
      .catch(() => {});
  }
}

export function setupBatchWizardCallbacks(bot: Bot, env: Env): void {
  bot.callbackQuery(/^bw:start$/, async (ctx) => {
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '📦' });
    await startBatchWizard(ctx, env);
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^bw:next$/, async (ctx) => {
    const userId = ctx.from?.id;
    if (!userId) return;

    const state = await getCache<WizardState>(env.DB, `bw:${userId}`);
    if (!state || !state.slug || !state.sourceSite) {
      await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({
        text: '⏱️ Session habis. Kirim /batch lagi.',
        show_alert: true,
      });
      return;
    }

    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '🔄' });

    const siteLabel =
      state.sourceSite === 'custom'
        ? `custom (${state.sourceSlug ?? '-'})`
        : state.sourceSite;
    const sourceLabel =
      state.sourceSite === 'custom'
        ? (state.customPattern ?? '-')
        : (state.sourceSlug ?? '-');

    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(
        ctx.chat!.id,
        ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!,
        `🔄 <b>Range Baru</b>\n\n` +
          `🎬 Repo: <code>${escapeHtml(state.slug)}</code>\n` +
          `🔗 Site: <b>${escapeHtml(siteLabel)}</b>\n` +
          `📼 Source: <code>${escapeHtml(sourceLabel)}</code>\n\n` +
          `Kirim <b>range episode</b> baru.\n` +
          `Contoh: <code>7-12</code> atau <code>13</code>`,
        {
          parse_mode: 'HTML',
          link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
          reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard().text('❌ Batal', 'bw:cancel'),
        }
      )
      .catch(() => {});
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^bw:go$/, async (ctx) => {
    const userId = ctx.from?.id;
    if (!userId) return;
    await execBatchWizard(ctx, env, userId);
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^bw:cancel$/, async (ctx) => {
    const userId = ctx.from?.id;
    if (userId) await deleteCache(env.DB, `bw:${userId}`);
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '🗑️ Dibatalkan' });
    await ctx
      .editMessageText('❌ <b>Batch dibatalkan.</b>', {
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        reply_markup: undefined,
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^bw:close$/, async (ctx) => {
    const userId = ctx.from?.id;
    if (userId) await deleteCache(env.DB, `bw:${userId}`);
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '🏠' });
    await ctx
      .editMessageReplyMarkup({ reply_markup: undefined })
      .catch(() => {});
  });
}

export const batchCommand: CommandDefinition = {
  name: 'batch',
  description: 'Batch scrape episode (wizard)',
  usage: '/batch',
  adminOnly: true,

  handler: async (ctx, env) => {
    const arg = typeof ctx.match === 'string' ? ctx.match.trim() : '';

    if (arg.toLowerCase() === 'reset') {
      const userId = ctx.from?.id;
      if (!userId) return;
      await deleteCache(env.DB, `bw:${userId}`);
      await ctx.reply('✅ Session batch di-reset.', { parse_mode: 'HTML' });
      return;
    }

    await startBatchWizard(ctx, env);
  },
};
===== END FILE =====

===== FILE: ./src/commands/database/actions.ts =====
// src/commands/database/actions.ts
import type { Context } from 'grammy';
import { InlineKeyboard, type Bot } from 'grammy';
import type { Env } from '../../types/env';
import type { D1Database } from '@cloudflare/workers-types';
import { escapeHtml } from '../../lib/utils';
import { createLazyInit } from '../../lib/lazy-init';
import {
  githubGetFile,
  githubCommitFile,
  githubDeleteFile,
  githubListDir,
} from '../../lib/github';
import {
  getCmsIndex,
  invalidateCmsIndex,
  type CmsIndex,
} from '../../lib/cms-cache';
import {
  splitContent,
  getFrontmatterField,
  applyEdits,
} from '../../lib/content-editor';
import {
  EDIT_FIELDS,
  findField,
  buildMainMenuKeyboard,
  buildDetailKeyboard,
  buildEditMenuKeyboard,
  buildValueKeyboard,
  buildFieldMenuText,
  buildValuePromptText,
} from './ui';

const SESSION_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;

type Step = 'idle' | 'awaiting_slug' | 'edit_menu' | 'awaiting_value' | 'awaiting_body';

interface Session {
  step: Step;
  slug?: string;
  activeField?: string;
  edits: Record<string, string>;
}

const ensureSessionDb = createLazyInit('DbSess', async (db) => {
  await db
    .prepare(
      `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS db_sessions (
        user_id      INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
        step         TEXT NOT NULL,
        slug         TEXT,
        active_field TEXT,
        edits_json   TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
        updated_at   INTEGER NOT NULL,
        expires_at   INTEGER NOT NULL
      )`
    )
    .run();
});

async function setSession(
  db: D1Database,
  userId: number,
  data: { step: Step; slug?: string; activeField?: string; edits?: Record<string, string> }
): Promise<void> {
  await ensureSessionDb(db);
  const now = Date.now();
  await db
    .prepare(
      `INSERT INTO db_sessions
         (user_id, step, slug, active_field, edits_json, updated_at, expires_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
       ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET
         step = excluded.step,
         slug = excluded.slug,
         active_field = excluded.active_field,
         edits_json = excluded.edits_json,
         updated_at = excluded.updated_at,
         expires_at = excluded.expires_at`
    )
    .bind(
      userId,
      data.step,
      data.slug ?? null,
      data.activeField ?? null,
      JSON.stringify(data.edits ?? {}),
      now,
      now + SESSION_TTL_MS
    )
    .run();
}

async function getSession(db: D1Database, userId: number): Promise<Session | null> {
  await ensureSessionDb(db);
  const row = await db
    .prepare(
      `SELECT step, slug, active_field, edits_json, expires_at
       FROM db_sessions WHERE user_id = ?`
    )
    .bind(userId)
    .first<{
      step: Step;
      slug: string | null;
      active_field: string | null;
      edits_json: string;
      expires_at: number;
    }>();

  if (!row) return null;
  if (row.expires_at < Date.now()) return null;

  let edits: Record<string, string> = {};
  try {
    edits = JSON.parse(row.edits_json);
  } catch {}

  return {
    step: row.step,
    slug: row.slug ?? undefined,
    activeField: row.active_field ?? undefined,
    edits,
  };
}

async function clearSession(db: D1Database, userId: number): Promise<void> {
  await ensureSessionDb(db);
  await db.prepare('DELETE FROM db_sessions WHERE user_id = ?').bind(userId).run();
}

export async function showMainMenu(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  edit = false
): Promise<void> {
  const loading = edit ? null : await ctx.reply('🔍 Loading index...');

  let index: CmsIndex;
  let fromCache: boolean;
  try {
    const r = await getCmsIndex(env);
    index = r.index;
    fromCache = r.fromCache;
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown';
    const text = `❌ Gagal load index:\n<code>${escapeHtml(msg.slice(0, 300))}</code>`;
    if (loading) {
      await ctx.api
        .editMessageText(ctx.chat!.id, loading.message_id, text, { parse_mode: 'HTML' })
        .catch(() => {});
    } else {
      await ctx.reply(text, { parse_mode: 'HTML' });
    }
    return;
  }

  if (loading) {
    await ctx.api.deleteMessage(ctx.chat!.id, loading.message_id).catch(() => {});
  }

  const ageStr = fromCache
    ? `🕐 Cache: ${Math.round((Date.now() - index.syncedAt) / 60000)} menit lalu`
    : '🆕 Fresh (baru sync)';

  const lines = [
    '🗄️ <b>Yukio Database</b>',
    '',
    `📚 Anime: <b>${index.animeSlugs.length}</b>`,
    `🎤 Actor files: <b>${index.actorLetters.length}</b>`,
    ageStr,
    '',
    '<i>Shortcut: <code>/database {slug}</code> langsung ke detail</i>',
  ];

  const kb = buildMainMenuKeyboard();
  const payload = {
    parse_mode: 'HTML' as const,
    link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
    reply_markup: kb,
  };

  if (edit && ctx.callbackQuery?.message?.message_id) {
    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(
        ctx.chat!.id,
        ctx.callbackQuery.message.message_id,
        lines.join('\n'),
        payload
      )
      .catch(() => {});
  } else {
    await ctx.reply(lines.join('\n'), payload);
  }
}

export async function showSlugPrompt(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  userId: number
): Promise<void> {
  await setSession(env.DB, userId, { step: 'awaiting_slug' });
  await ctx.api
    .editMessageText(
      ctx.chat!.id,
      ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!,
      '🔍 <b>Cari Anime</b>\n\n' +
        'Kirim <b>slug</b> anime.\n' +
        '<i>Contoh: <code>jujutsu-kaisen</code></i>',
      {
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
        reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard().text('❌ Batal', 'db:home'),
      }
    )
    .catch(() => {});
}

export async function handleSlugInput(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  userId: number,
  rawSlug: string
): Promise<void> {
  const slug = rawSlug.toLowerCase().trim();

  if (!/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*[a-z0-9]$/.test(slug)) {
    await ctx.reply(
      '❌ Slug invalid. Format: <code>lowercase-with-dash</code>\n\nCoba lagi:',
      { parse_mode: 'HTML' }
    );
    return;
  }

  await clearSession(env.DB, userId);

  const loading = await ctx.reply(`🔍 Cek <code>${escapeHtml(slug)}</code>...`, {
    parse_mode: 'HTML',
  });

  const file = await githubGetFile(env, `src/content/anime/${slug}.md`).catch(() => null);

  await ctx.api.deleteMessage(ctx.chat!.id, loading.message_id).catch(() => {});

  if (!file) {
    await ctx.reply(
      `❌ <b>Slug tidak ditemukan</b>\n\n` +
        `<code>${escapeHtml(slug)}</code> belum ada di yukio-data.\n\n` +
        `<i>Pakai /anime untuk tambah baru.</i>`,
      {
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
        reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard()
          .text('🔍 Coba Lagi', 'db:search')
          .text('◀️ Kembali', 'db:home'),
      }
    );
    return;
  }

  await showDetailFromContent(ctx, env, slug, file.content, false);
}

export async function showDetail(ctx: Context, env: Env, slug: string): Promise<void> {
  const file = await githubGetFile(env, `src/content/anime/${slug}.md`).catch(() => null);

  if (!file) {
    await ctx
      .answerCallbackQuery({ text: '❌ File tidak ditemukan', show_alert: true })
      .catch(() => {});
    return;
  }

  await showDetailFromContent(ctx, env, slug, file.content, true);
}

async function showDetailFromContent(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  slug: string,
  content: string,
  edit: boolean
): Promise<void> {
  const { frontmatter } = splitContent(content);
  const get = (key: string): string | null => getFrontmatterField(frontmatter, key);

  const title = get('title') ?? slug;
  const type = get('type') ?? '-';
  const status = get('status') ?? '-';
  const year = get('year') ?? '-';
  const episodes = get('episodes') ?? '-';
  const duration = get('duration') ?? '-';
  const malId = get('malId') ?? '-';

  const { index } = await getCmsIndex(env);
  const folder = index.animeFolders[slug];
  const hasChars = folder?.characters ?? false;
  const hasEps = folder?.episodes ?? false;
  const hasStreams = folder?.episodeStreams ?? false;
  const hasFr = folder?.franchises ?? false;

  const lines = [
    `📄 <code>${escapeHtml(slug)}</code>`,
    '',
    `<b>${escapeHtml(title)}</b>`,
    `🎬 ${type} · ${status}`,
    `📅 ${year} · 📼 ${episodes} ep · ⏱️ ${duration} min`,
    `🆔 MAL: ${malId}`,
    '',
    `<i>Data folder:</i>`,
    `  ${hasChars ? '✅' : '⬜'} Characters`,
    `  ${hasEps ? '✅' : '⬜'} Episodes (metadata)`,
    `  ${hasStreams ? '✅' : '⬜'} Episodes (streams)`,
    `  ${hasFr ? '✅' : '⬜'} Franchises`,
  ];

  const kb = buildDetailKeyboard(slug, hasChars, hasEps, hasStreams, hasFr);
  const payload = {
    parse_mode: 'HTML' as const,
    link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
    reply_markup: kb,
  };

  if (edit && ctx.callbackQuery?.message?.message_id) {
    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(
        ctx.chat!.id,
        ctx.callbackQuery.message.message_id,
        lines.join('\n'),
        payload
      )
      .catch(() => {});
  } else {
    await ctx.reply(lines.join('\n'), payload);
  }
}

async function showEditMenu(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  userId: number,
  slug: string,
  edits: Record<string, string>
): Promise<void> {
  await setSession(env.DB, userId, { step: 'edit_menu', slug, edits });

  const text = buildFieldMenuText(slug, edits);
  const kb = buildEditMenuKeyboard(slug, edits);

  await ctx.api
    .editMessageText(
      ctx.chat!.id,
      ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!,
      text,
      {
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
        reply_markup: kb,
      }
    )
    .catch(() => {});
}

async function showFieldPrompt(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  userId: number,
  slug: string,
  fieldKey: string,
  edits: Record<string, string>
): Promise<void> {
  const field = findField(fieldKey);
  if (!field) {
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '❌ Field tidak dikenal' });
    return;
  }

  const file = await githubGetFile(env, `src/content/anime/${slug}.md`).catch(() => null);
  if (!file) {
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '❌ File hilang', show_alert: true });
    return;
  }

  const { frontmatter } = splitContent(file.content);
  const current = getFrontmatterField(frontmatter, fieldKey);

  const step: Step = field.type === 'body' ? 'awaiting_body' : 'awaiting_value';
  await setSession(env.DB, userId, { step, slug, activeField: fieldKey, edits });

  const text = buildValuePromptText(slug, field, current);
  const kb =
    field.type === 'choice' && field.choices
      ? buildValueKeyboard(fieldKey, field.choices)
      : new InlineKeyboard().text('↩️ Balik', 'db:edit:back');

  await ctx.api
    .editMessageText(ctx.chat!.id, ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!, text, {
      parse_mode: 'HTML',
      link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
      reply_markup: kb,
    })
    .catch(() => {});
}

async function saveEdits(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  userId: number,
  slug: string,
  edits: Record<string, string>
): Promise<void> {
  const count = Object.keys(edits).length;
  if (count === 0) {
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '❌ Belum ada perubahan' });
    return;
  }

  await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '📤 Saving...' });

  const loadingMsgId = ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!;
  await ctx.api
    .editMessageText(
      ctx.chat!.id,
      loadingMsgId,
      `📤 <b>Menyimpan ${count} perubahan...</b>\n\n🆔 <code>${escapeHtml(slug)}</code>`,
      { parse_mode: 'HTML', link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true } }
    )
    .catch(() => {});

  try {
    const file = await githubGetFile(env, `src/content/anime/${slug}.md`);
    if (!file) throw new Error('File MD tidak ditemukan');

    const newContent = applyEdits(file.content, edits);

    const result = await githubCommitFile(
      env,
      `src/content/anime/${slug}.md`,
      newContent,
      `chore(cms): update ${slug} (${count} field)`
    );

    if (!result.ok) throw new Error(result.error ?? 'push failed');

    await clearSession(env.DB, userId);
    await invalidateCmsIndex(env);

    const lines = [
      `✅ <b>Update berhasil</b>`,
      '',
      `🆔 <code>${escapeHtml(slug)}</code>`,
      `📦 ${count} field diubah`,
      `🔗 Commit: <code>${result.sha?.slice(0, 7) ?? '?'}</code>`,
      '',
      `<i>Deploy ~2 menit</i>`,
    ];

    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(ctx.chat!.id, loadingMsgId, lines.join('\n'), {
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
        reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard()
          .text('✏️ Edit Lagi', `db:edit:${slug}`)
          .text('📄 Detail', `db:view:${slug}`),
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown';
    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(
        ctx.chat!.id,
        loadingMsgId,
        `❌ <b>Gagal save</b>\n\n<code>${escapeHtml(msg.slice(0, 300))}</code>`,
        {
          parse_mode: 'HTML',
          reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard().text('◀️ Balik', `db:edit:${slug}`),
        }
      )
      .catch(() => {});
  }
}

async function listJsonFiles(env: Env, path: string): Promise<string[]> {
  try {
    const dir = await githubListDir(env, path);
    return dir
      .filter((d) => d.type === 'file' && d.name.endsWith('.json'))
      .map((d) => d.name)
      .sort();
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

async function showEpisodesMenu(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  slug: string
): Promise<void> {
  const [metaFiles, streamFiles] = await Promise.all([
    listJsonFiles(env, `data/anime/${slug}/episodes`),
    listJsonFiles(env, `data/anime/${slug}/episodes/streams`),
  ]);

  const lines = [
    `🎬 <b>Episodes — ${escapeHtml(slug)}</b>`,
    '',
    `📄 <b>Metadata</b> (${metaFiles.length}):`,
  ];
  if (metaFiles.length === 0) lines.push('  <i>(kosong)</i>');
  else for (const f of metaFiles) lines.push(`  • <code>${escapeHtml(f)}</code>`);

  lines.push('');
  lines.push(`📺 <b>Streams</b> (${streamFiles.length}):`);
  if (streamFiles.length === 0) lines.push('  <i>(kosong)</i>');
  else for (const f of streamFiles) lines.push(`  • <code>${escapeHtml(f)}</code>`);

  lines.push('');
  lines.push('<i>Tap file untuk preview / hapus:</i>');

  const kb = new InlineKeyboard();
  for (const f of metaFiles) kb.text(`📄 ${f}`, `db:epv:${slug}:meta:${f}`).row();
  for (const f of streamFiles) kb.text(`📺 ${f}`, `db:epv:${slug}:streams:${f}`).row();

  kb.text('🔍 Refresh', `db:ep:${slug}`).text('◀️ Detail', `db:view:${slug}`);

  await ctx.api
    .editMessageText(
      ctx.chat!.id,
      ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!,
      lines.join('\n'),
      {
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
        reply_markup: kb,
      }
    )
    .catch(() => {});
}

async function showChunkPreview(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  slug: string,
  type: 'meta' | 'streams',
  fileName: string
): Promise<void> {
  const path =
    type === 'meta'
      ? `data/anime/${slug}/episodes/${fileName}`
      : `data/anime/${slug}/episodes/streams/${fileName}`;

  const file = await githubGetFile(env, path).catch(() => null);
  if (!file) {
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '❌ File hilang', show_alert: true });
    return;
  }

  let preview = file.content;
  if (preview.length > 3000) preview = preview.slice(0, 3000) + '\n\n… [truncated]';

  const sizeKb = Math.round(file.content.length / 1024);
  const typeLabel = type === 'meta' ? '📄 Metadata' : '📺 Streams';

  const lines = [
    `${typeLabel} — <code>${escapeHtml(fileName)}</code>`,
    '',
    `📏 ${sizeKb} KB`,
    '',
    `<pre>${escapeHtml(preview)}</pre>`,
  ];

  await ctx.api
    .editMessageText(
      ctx.chat!.id,
      ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!,
      lines.join('\n'),
      {
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
        reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard()
          .text('🗑️ Hapus', `db:epd:${slug}:${type}:${fileName}`)
          .row()
          .text('◀️ Kembali', `db:ep:${slug}`),
      }
    )
    .catch(() => {});
}

async function execDeleteChunk(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  slug: string,
  type: 'meta' | 'streams',
  fileName: string
): Promise<void> {
  const path =
    type === 'meta'
      ? `data/anime/${slug}/episodes/${fileName}`
      : `data/anime/${slug}/episodes/streams/${fileName}`;

  await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '🗑️ Deleting...' });
  const loadingMsgId = ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!;

  const result = await githubDeleteFile(env, path, `chore(cms): delete ${fileName}`);
  if (!result.ok) {
    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(
        ctx.chat!.id,
        loadingMsgId,
        `❌ <b>Gagal hapus</b>\n\n<code>${escapeHtml((result.error ?? 'unknown').slice(0, 200))}</code>`,
        {
          parse_mode: 'HTML',
          reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard().text('◀️ Kembali', `db:ep:${slug}`),
        }
      )
      .catch(() => {});
    return;
  }

  await invalidateCmsIndex(env);
  await showEpisodesMenu(ctx, env, slug);
}

async function collectFolderFiles(
  env: Env,
  path: string,
  depth = 0
): Promise<string[]> {
  if (depth > 3) return [];
  const files: string[] = [];
  try {
    const items = await githubListDir(env, path);
    for (const item of items) {
      const child = `${path}/${item.name}`;
      if (item.type === 'file') files.push(child);
      else if (item.type === 'dir') files.push(...(await collectFolderFiles(env, child, depth + 1)));
    }
  } catch {}
  return files;
}

async function showDeleteConfirm(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  slug: string
): Promise<void> {
  await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '🔍 Scan file...' });

  const mdPath = `src/content/anime/${slug}.md`;
  const md = await githubGetFile(env, mdPath).catch(() => null);
  const dataFiles = await collectFolderFiles(env, `data/anime/${slug}`, 0);
  const total = (md ? 1 : 0) + dataFiles.length;

  if (total === 0) {
    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(
        ctx.chat!.id,
        ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!,
        `ℹ️ <b>Tidak ada file untuk dihapus</b>\n\n🆔 <code>${escapeHtml(slug)}</code>`,
        {
          parse_mode: 'HTML',
          reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard().text('◀️ Detail', `db:view:${slug}`),
        }
      )
      .catch(() => {});
    return;
  }

  const lines = [
    `⚠️ <b>Konfirmasi Hapus</b>`,
    '',
    `🆔 <code>${escapeHtml(slug)}</code>`,
    '',
    `<b>${total} file akan dihapus:</b>`,
  ];
  if (md) lines.push(`  📄 <code>${escapeHtml(mdPath)}</code>`);
  for (const f of dataFiles.slice(0, 10)) lines.push(`  📁 <code>${escapeHtml(f)}</code>`);
  if (dataFiles.length > 10) lines.push(`  <i>… dan ${dataFiles.length - 10} file lain</i>`);
  lines.push('');
  lines.push('<b>⚠️ Tidak bisa dibatalkan.</b>');

  await ctx.api
    .editMessageText(
      ctx.chat!.id,
      ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!,
      lines.join('\n'),
      {
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
        reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard()
          .text(`✅ Hapus ${total} file`, `db:dely:${slug}`)
          .row()
          .text('❌ Batal', `db:view:${slug}`),
      }
    )
    .catch(() => {});
}

async function execDeleteAnime(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env,
  slug: string
): Promise<void> {
  await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '🗑️ Deleting...' });
  const loadingMsgId = ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!;

  try {
    const mdPath = `src/content/anime/${slug}.md`;
    const md = await githubGetFile(env, mdPath).catch(() => null);
    const dataFiles = await collectFolderFiles(env, `data/anime/${slug}`, 0);
    const paths = [...(md ? [mdPath] : []), ...dataFiles];

    if (paths.length === 0) throw new Error('Tidak ada file');

    let deleted = 0;
    const errors: string[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < paths.length; i++) {
      const p = paths[i]!;
      try {
        await ctx.api
          .editMessageText(
            ctx.chat!.id,
            loadingMsgId,
            `🗑️ <b>[${i + 1}/${paths.length}]</b>\n\n<code>${escapeHtml(p)}</code>`,
            { parse_mode: 'HTML', link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true } }
          )
          .catch(() => {});
        const r = await githubDeleteFile(env, p, `chore(cms): delete ${slug}`);
        if (r.ok) deleted++;
        else errors.push(`${p}: ${r.error ?? 'unknown'}`);
      } catch (err) {
        errors.push(`${p}: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown'}`);
      }
    }

    await invalidateCmsIndex(env);

    const lines = [
      `✅ <b>Hapus selesai</b>`,
      '',
      `🆔 <code>${escapeHtml(slug)}</code>`,
      `🗑️ Dihapus: <b>${deleted}</b> / ${paths.length} file`,
    ];
    if (errors.length > 0) {
      lines.push('');
      lines.push(`⚠️ <b>Error (${errors.length}):</b>`);
      for (const e of errors.slice(0, 3)) lines.push(`• <code>${escapeHtml(e.slice(0, 120))}</code>`);
    }

    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(ctx.chat!.id, loadingMsgId, lines.join('\n'), {
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
        reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard()
          .text('🔍 Cari Lagi', 'db:search')
          .text('🏠 Menu', 'db:home'),
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown';
    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(
        ctx.chat!.id,
        loadingMsgId,
        `❌ <b>Gagal hapus</b>\n\n<code>${escapeHtml(msg.slice(0, 300))}</code>`,
        {
          parse_mode: 'HTML',
          reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard().text('◀️ Detail', `db:view:${slug}`),
        }
      )
      .catch(() => {});
  }
}

async function showStats(ctx: Context, env: Env): Promise<void> {
  const { index } = await getCmsIndex(env);

  let withChars = 0;
  let withEps = 0;
  let withStreams = 0;
  let withFr = 0;

  for (const slug of index.animeSlugs) {
    const f = index.animeFolders[slug];
    if (f?.characters) withChars++;
    if (f?.episodes) withEps++;
    if (f?.episodeStreams) withStreams++;
    if (f?.franchises) withFr++;
  }

  const total = index.animeSlugs.length;
  const pct = (n: number) => ((n / total) * 100).toFixed(1);

  const lines = [
    '📊 <b>Repo Stats</b>',
    '',
    `📄 Anime MD: <b>${total}</b>`,
    `🎤 Actor files: <b>${index.actorLetters.length}</b>`,
    '',
    '<b>Kelengkapan data:</b>',
    `  👥 Characters: <b>${withChars}</b> (${pct(withChars)}%)`,
    `  📄 Episodes meta: <b>${withEps}</b> (${pct(withEps)}%)`,
    `  📺 Episodes streams: <b>${withStreams}</b> (${pct(withStreams)}%)`,
    `  🔗 Franchises: <b>${withFr}</b> (${pct(withFr)}%)`,
    '',
    '<b>⚠️ Belum lengkap:</b>',
    `  Tanpa characters: <b>${total - withChars}</b>`,
    `  Tanpa episodes meta: <b>${total - withEps}</b>`,
    `  Tanpa streams: <b>${total - withStreams}</b>`,
    `  Tanpa franchises: <b>${total - withFr}</b>`,
  ];

  await ctx.api
    .editMessageText(
      ctx.chat!.id,
      ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!,
      lines.join('\n'),
      {
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
        reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard().text('◀️ Kembali', 'db:home'),
      }
    )
    .catch(() => {});
}

async function doSync(ctx: Context, env: Env): Promise<void> {
  await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '🔄 Syncing...' });
  try {
    await invalidateCmsIndex(env);
    const { index } = await getCmsIndex(env, true);
    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(
        ctx.chat!.id,
        ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!,
        `✅ <b>Sync selesai</b>\n\n` +
          `📚 Anime: <b>${index.animeSlugs.length}</b>\n` +
          `🎤 Actor files: <b>${index.actorLetters.length}</b>`,
        {
          parse_mode: 'HTML',
          link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
          reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard().text('◀️ Kembali', 'db:home'),
        }
      )
      .catch(() => {});
  } catch (err) {
    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown';
    await ctx.api
      .editMessageText(
        ctx.chat!.id,
        ctx.callbackQuery!.message!.message_id!,
        `❌ <b>Sync gagal</b>\n\n<code>${escapeHtml(msg.slice(0, 300))}</code>`,
        {
          parse_mode: 'HTML',
          reply_markup: new InlineKeyboard().text('◀️ Kembali', 'db:home'),
        }
      )
      .catch(() => {});
  }
}

export async function handleDatabaseText(
  ctx: Context,
  env: Env
): Promise<boolean> {
  const userId = ctx.from?.id;
  if (!userId) return false;

  const text = ctx.message?.text ?? '';
  if (!text || text.startsWith('/')) return false;

  const session = await getSession(env.DB, userId);
  if (!session) return false;

  if (session.step === 'awaiting_slug') {
    await handleSlugInput(ctx, env, userId, text);
    return true;
  }

  if (session.step === 'awaiting_value' || session.step === 'awaiting_body') {
    const { slug, activeField } = session;
    if (!slug || !activeField) {
      await clearSession(env.DB, userId);
      return false;
    }

    const field = findField(activeField);
    if (!field) {
      await clearSession(env.DB, userId);
      return false;
    }

    const isBody = session.step === 'awaiting_body';
    const raw = isBody ? text : text.trim();

    if (!isBody) {
      if (field.type === 'number' && isNaN(Number(raw))) {
        await ctx.reply('❌ Harus angka. Coba lagi:');
        return true;
      }
      if (field.type === 'url') {
        try {
          new URL(raw);
        } catch {
          await ctx.reply('❌ URL tidak valid. Coba lagi:');
          return true;
        }
      }
    } else if (raw.trim().length < 10) {
      await ctx.reply('❌ Sinopsis minimal 10 karakter. Coba lagi:');
      return true;
    }

    const newEdits = { ...session.edits, [activeField]: raw };
    await setSession(env.DB, userId, {
      step: 'edit_menu',
      slug,
      edits: newEdits,
    });

    const editMsg = await ctx.reply('✅ Tersimpan. Kembali ke menu edit...', {
      parse_mode: 'HTML',
    });
    void editMsg;

    const lines = buildFieldMenuText(slug, newEdits);
    const kb = buildEditMenuKeyboard(slug, newEdits);
    await ctx.reply(lines, {
      parse_mode: 'HTML',
      link_preview_options: { is_disabled: true },
      reply_markup: kb,
    });
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

export function setupDatabaseCallbacks(bot: Bot, env: Env): void {
  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:home$/, async (ctx) => {
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery().catch(() => {});
    await showMainMenu(ctx, env, true);
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:search$/, async (ctx) => {
    const userId = ctx.from?.id;
    if (!userId) return;
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery().catch(() => {});
    await showSlugPrompt(ctx, env, userId);
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:view:(.+)$/, async (ctx) => {
    const slug = ctx.match[1] ?? '';
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery().catch(() => {});
    await showDetail(ctx, env, slug);
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:stats$/, async (ctx) => {
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery().catch(() => {});
    await showStats(ctx, env);
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:sync$/, async (ctx) => {
    await doSync(ctx, env);
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:del:(.+)$/, async (ctx) => {
    const slug = ctx.match[1] ?? '';
    await showDeleteConfirm(ctx, env, slug);
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:dely:(.+)$/, async (ctx) => {
    const slug = ctx.match[1] ?? '';
    await execDeleteAnime(ctx, env, slug);
  });

  /* Episodes */
  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:ep:(.+)$/, async (ctx) => {
    const slug = ctx.match[1] ?? '';
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery().catch(() => {});
    await showEpisodesMenu(ctx, env, slug);
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:epv:([^:]+):(meta|streams):([^:]+)$/, async (ctx) => {
    const slug = ctx.match[1] ?? '';
    const type = (ctx.match[2] ?? 'meta') as 'meta' | 'streams';
    const fileName = ctx.match[3] ?? '';
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery().catch(() => {});
    await showChunkPreview(ctx, env, slug, type, fileName);
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:epd:([^:]+):(meta|streams):([^:]+)$/, async (ctx) => {
    const slug = ctx.match[1] ?? '';
    const type = (ctx.match[2] ?? 'meta') as 'meta' | 'streams';
    const fileName = ctx.match[3] ?? '';
    await execDeleteChunk(ctx, env, slug, type, fileName);
  });

  /* Edit */
  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:edit:(.+)$/, async (ctx) => {
    const userId = ctx.from?.id;
    if (!userId) return;
    const slug = ctx.match[1] ?? '';
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery().catch(() => {});
    await showEditMenu(ctx, env, userId, slug, {});
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:edit:back$/, async (ctx) => {
    const userId = ctx.from?.id;
    if (!userId) return;
    const session = await getSession(env.DB, userId);
    if (!session?.slug) {
      await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '⏱️ Session habis', show_alert: true });
      return;
    }
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery().catch(() => {});
    await showEditMenu(ctx, env, userId, session.slug, session.edits);
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:ef:([a-zA-Z.]+)$/, async (ctx) => {
    const userId = ctx.from?.id;
    if (!userId) return;
    const key = ctx.match[1] ?? '';
    const session = await getSession(env.DB, userId);
    if (!session?.slug) {
      await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '⏱️ Session habis', show_alert: true });
      return;
    }
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery().catch(() => {});
    await showFieldPrompt(ctx, env, userId, session.slug, key, session.edits);
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:ev:(\d+)$/, async (ctx) => {
    const userId = ctx.from?.id;
    if (!userId) return;
    const idx = parseInt(ctx.match[1] ?? '-1', 10);
    const session = await getSession(env.DB, userId);
    if (!session?.slug || !session.activeField) {
      await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '⏱️ Session habis', show_alert: true });
      return;
    }

    const field = findField(session.activeField);
    if (!field || !field.choices || idx < 0 || idx >= field.choices.length) {
      await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '❌ Pilihan invalid' });
      return;
    }

    const value = field.choices[idx]!;
    await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: `✅ ${value}` });

    const newEdits = { ...session.edits, [session.activeField]: value };
    await setSession(env.DB, userId, {
      step: 'edit_menu',
      slug: session.slug,
      edits: newEdits,
    });

    await showEditMenu(ctx, env, userId, session.slug, newEdits);
  });

  bot.callbackQuery(/^db:save$/, async (ctx) => {
    const userId = ctx.from?.id;
    if (!userId) return;
    const session = await getSession(env.DB, userId);
    if (!session?.slug) {
      await ctx.answerCallbackQuery({ text: '⏱️ Session habis', show_alert: true });
      return;
    }
    await saveEdits(ctx, env, userId, session.slug, session.edits);
  });
}

void EDIT_FIELDS;

// src/commands/database/index.ts
import type { CommandDefinition } from '../registry';
import type { Env } from '../../types/env';
import { showMainMenu, handleSlugInput } from './actions';

export const databaseCommand: CommandDefinition = {
  name: 'database',
  description: 'CMS yukio-data — cek slug, edit, hapus',
  usage: '/database [slug]',
  adminOnly: true,

  handler: async (ctx, env: Env) => {
    const arg = typeof ctx.match === 'string' ? ctx.match.trim() : '';
    const userId = ctx.from?.id;
    if (!userId) return;

    if (arg) {
      await handleSlugInput(ctx, env, userId, arg);
      return;
    }

    await showMainMenu(ctx, env);
  },
};

===== FILE: ./src/commands/database/ui.ts =====
import { InlineKeyboard } from 'grammy';
import { escapeHtml } from '../../lib/utils';

export interface FieldDef {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  type: 'text' | 'number' | 'choice' | 'csv' | 'url' | 'body';
  choices?: string[];
  hint?: string;
}

export const EDIT_FIELDS: FieldDef[] = [
  { key: 'title', label: '📄 Title', type: 'text' },
  { key: 'titleEnglish', label: '📄 Title EN', type: 'text' },
  { key: 'titleNative', label: '📄 Title JP', type: 'text' },
  {
    key: 'type',
    label: '🎬 Type',
    type: 'choice',
    choices: ['TV', 'Movie', 'OVA', 'ONA', 'Special', 'Music', 'Unknown'],
  },
  {
    key: 'status',
    label: '📊 Status',
    type: 'choice',
    choices: ['airing', 'finished', 'upcoming', 'hiatus', 'cancelled'],
  },
  { key: 'source', label: '📖 Source', type: 'text' },
  {
    key: 'season',
    label: '🌤️ Season',
    type: 'choice',
    choices: ['winter', 'spring', 'summer', 'fall'],
  },
  { key: 'year', label: '📅 Year', type: 'number', hint: '1900-2100' },
  { key: 'episodes', label: '📼 Episodes', type: 'number' },
  { key: 'duration', label: '⏱️ Duration', type: 'number', hint: 'menit' },
  {
    key: 'rating',
    label: '🔞 Age Rating',
    type: 'choice',
    choices: ['G', 'PG', 'PG-13', 'R', 'R+', 'Rx'],
  },
  { key: 'stats.score', label: '⭐ Score', type: 'number', hint: '0.0 - 10.0' },
  { key: 'malId', label: '🆔 MAL ID', type: 'number' },
  { key: 'anilistId', label: '🆔 AniList ID', type: 'number' },
  { key: 'genres', label: '🏷️ Genres', type: 'csv', hint: 'action, comedy' },
  { key: 'studios', label: '🏢 Studios', type: 'csv', hint: 'mappa, bones' },
  { key: 'image', label: '🖼️ Image', type: 'url' },
  { key: 'banner', label: '🖼️ Banner', type: 'url' },
  { key: 'trailer', label: '🎬 Trailer', type: 'text' },
  { key: 'body', label: '📝 Sinopsis', type: 'body' },
];

export function findField(key: string): FieldDef | null {
  return EDIT_FIELDS.find((f) => f.key === key) ?? null;
}

export function buildMainMenuKeyboard(): InlineKeyboard {
  return new InlineKeyboard()
    .text('🔍 Cari Slug', 'db:search')
    .text('📊 Stats', 'db:stats')
    .row()
    .text('🔄 Sync', 'db:sync');
}

export function buildDetailKeyboard(
  slug: string,
  hasChars: boolean,
  hasEps: boolean,
  hasStreams: boolean,
  hasFr: boolean
): InlineKeyboard {
  void hasChars;
  void hasEps;
  void hasFr;

  const kb = new InlineKeyboard()
    .text('✏️ Edit', `db:edit:${slug}`)
    .text('🗑️ Hapus', `db:del:${slug}`)
    .row();

  if (hasStreams) {
    kb.text('🎬 Episodes', `db:ep:${slug}`).row();
  }

  kb.text('🔍 Cari Lagi', 'db:search').text('◀️ Menu', 'db:home');

  return kb;
}

export function buildEditMenuKeyboard(
  slug: string,
  edits: Record<string, string>
): InlineKeyboard {
  const kb = new InlineKeyboard();
  let i = 0;
  for (const f of EDIT_FIELDS) {
    const mark = edits[f.key] !== undefined ? '✅' : '⬜';
    kb.text(`${mark} ${f.label}`, `db:ef:${f.key}`);
    i++;
    if (i % 2 === 0) kb.row();
  }
  if (i % 2 !== 0) kb.row();

  kb.text(`💾 Save (${Object.keys(edits).length})`, 'db:save');
  kb.text('❌ Batal', `db:view:${slug}`);
  kb.row();
  kb.text('◀️ Detail', `db:view:${slug}`);

  return kb;
}

export function buildValueKeyboard(
  fieldKey: string,
  choices: string[]
): InlineKeyboard {
  const kb = new InlineKeyboard();
  choices.forEach((c, i) => {
    kb.text(c, `db:ev:${i}`);
    if ((i + 1) % 3 === 0) kb.row();
  });
  if (choices.length % 3 !== 0) kb.row();
  kb.text('↩️ Balik', 'db:edit:back');
  void fieldKey;
  return kb;
}

export function buildFieldMenuText(
  slug: string,
  edits: Record<string, string>
): string {
  const count = Object.keys(edits).length;
  const lines: string[] = [];
  lines.push(`✏️ <b>Edit — ${escapeHtml(slug)}</b>`);
  lines.push('');

  if (count === 0) {
    lines.push('<i>Belum ada perubahan.</i>');
  } else {
    lines.push(`<b>📝 Perubahan pending (${count}):</b>`);
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(edits)) {
      const f = findField(k);
      const label = f?.label ?? k;
      const preview =
        k === 'body'
          ? `${v.slice(0, 40).replace(/\n/g, ' ')}…`
          : v.length > 40
            ? v.slice(0, 38) + '…'
            : v;
      lines.push(`• ${label} → <code>${escapeHtml(preview)}</code>`);
    }
  }

  lines.push('');
  lines.push('<i>Tap field untuk ubah:</i>');
  return lines.join('\n');
}

export function buildValuePromptText(
  slug: string,
  field: FieldDef,
  current: string | null
): string {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  lines.push(`✏️ <b>Edit: ${escapeHtml(field.label)}</b>`);
  lines.push('');
  lines.push(`🆔 <code>${escapeHtml(slug)}</code>`);

  if (current !== null && current !== '') {
    const cur = current.length > 200 ? current.slice(0, 200) + '…' : current;
    lines.push(`📌 Sekarang: <code>${escapeHtml(cur)}</code>`);
  } else {
    lines.push(`📌 Sekarang: <i>(kosong)</i>`);
  }
  lines.push('');

  if (field.type === 'choice' && field.choices) {
    lines.push('<i>Pilih dari tombol di bawah:</i>');
  } else if (field.type === 'url') {
    lines.push('<i>Kirim URL baru (https://...):</i>');
  } else if (field.type === 'body') {
    lines.push('<i>Kirim isi body baru (sinopsis, multi-baris):</i>');
  } else if (field.hint) {
    lines.push(`<i>Kirim value baru. Format: ${escapeHtml(field.hint)}</i>`);
  } else {
    lines.push('<i>Kirim value baru:</i>');
  }

  return lines.join('\n');
}
